Of the Threefold Life. Chapter 9.

# The Ninth Chapter

## Concerning the Threefold Life

<margin><sup>s</sup> Driving or Impulsion.</margin>
### Also of the <sup>s</sup> *Inclination and whole Government of Man in this World.*

#### *Highly to be Considered.*

It is shown to me what the Devil's intention is: how he will endeavor to smother these high and precious writings. Therefore, be watchful, you children of God; *do not believe the sophisters* <note>original: "Sophifters"; in this context, it refers to academic theologians or those who use clever but deceptive arguments to maintain their status.</note> *too much*, who cry out, "Heretic, heretic, to the fire with them!" That is not the voice of the Holy Spirit, but the voice of the Antichrist and of the dragon. For these writings will thoroughly reveal the Devil’s smoking pit; and not only so, but they also show the *Whore of Babylon* <note>original: "Whore of Babell"; a biblical figure from Revelation used by reformers to symbolize religious corruption.</note> very plainly, like a whore <margin><sup>t</sup> Exposed in the pillory <note>original: "Am Pranger"; a German expression meaning to be publicly shamed or placed in a pillory.</note></margin> <sup>t</sup> put to open shame.

2. But (because the men of the spirit of this world take care *only* for their bellies, and are loath to lose their honor, credit, reputation, and goods, but would rather part with God and the kingdom of heaven), we shall be persecuted by the *Whore* through the instigation of the devils. Therefore, be watchful, you children of God, and do not look upon that which is high and has great authority, but regard the welfare of your souls. This we leave you as a last farewell.

3. Christ says: *No one lights a lamp and puts it under a cover or under a basket, but sets it upon a table, so that all who are in the house may see by its light.* Thus we must do also, and must not bury our talent (which is so dearly bestowed upon us) in the earth; for we must give an account of it at the Day of God’s Judgment, as the *Spirit of the Mother* <note>The "Spirit of the Mother" in Boehme's work often refers to the divine Wisdom or the generative source of spiritual life.</note> suggests to us.

<margin><sup>u</sup> Which is written here.</margin>
4. If this knowledge [or understanding] of *this spirit* shall happen to fall upon anyone, they will then be *certain* of what it is <sup>u</sup>. We need no letters of recommendation; Christ is our letter of recommendation, which is sufficient for us. No one ought to call themselves after *my name*; all of us who know Christ put him on and are members of *his body*. We call ourselves Christians and children of God, and brothers and sisters of one another.

5. Therefore...

<summary>In this opening to Chapter 9, Boehme warns that his writings will be attacked by religious authorities ("the Whore of Babylon") and worldly scholars. He encourages believers to value their spiritual well-being over worldly status and to share the divine light revealed in these texts.</summary>

<meta>The author continues describing the conflict between the soul and the influences of both the devil and the material world, emphasizing how the "Spirit of this world" seeks to express its hidden wonders through human beings.</meta>

<header>Chapter 9: Of the Threefold Life.</header>

5. Therefore, now when we consider the governance [or course] of our life, we find a powerful struggle within it, which the Devil has with the soul, and also which the Spirit of this world <note>original: "Spirit of this world"; in Boehme's theology, this refers to the governing physical and psychological energy of the created universe, distinct from the Holy Spirit.</note> has with the soul. For there is a knowledge in the Spirit of this world; indeed, there is no divine understanding in it, but there is a perception planted in the Matrix <term>original: "Matrix"; the creative womb or source from which nature is produced.</term>, that is, in the Center of Nature.

6. For this world, before the Creation, stood from eternity in the Eternal Wisdom as an invisible figure. It is now created as a distinct *principle* <note>original: "Principle"; here meaning a fundamental realm or state of existence.</note> of its own, so that it might bring all its wonders and works into substance, so that they may appear in *their proper form* after the time [of this world] has passed.

7. And so there is a natural struggle within humanity regarding this, for no creature *but humanity* can bring the wonders of the world to light. Therefore, the Spirit of this world has also longed so very much for humanity and has drawn humanity to itself, so that it might show forth its wonders through us—that humanity should produce all arts and languages <margin>* The Spirit of this world.</margin> in it. Moreover, the Spirit and Heart out of the earth and metals—namely, that precious stone, the *Philosophers’ Stone* <note>A legendary substance in alchemy capable of transforming base metals into gold; here, it symbolizes the hidden spiritual perfection within physical matter.</note>—has indeed been found by few since *Solomon's time*. But now, at the end of time, it shall be found more clearly, as we know and understand.

8. For whoever rightly understands our writings concerning the Center of Nature, and its progress to the "Number-Three" <note>A reference to the Trinity.</note> upon the Cross and to the brilliance of the Majesty, may well find it in metals. It is not difficult, if he only learns the *right entrance*; he then has <margin>Note.</margin> the end at hand. We shall not mention that further here, for it belongs to the *Magi* <term>original: "Magi"; spiritually gifted individuals who understand the hidden mysteries of nature.</term> who are born *magically*. We speak only of the foundation of nature and of the Spirit of this world.

9. And we declare to you that the Spirit of this world is created with such an inclination, and that it has a natural will to reveal itself and all its *mysteries*. We see this before our eyes by what it has built or brought forth—how it has established a dominion and kingdom upon earth.

10. Just look at the actions of humanity, from the highest to the <margin>the course.</margin> lowest: the Spirit of this world has thus built the whole order of them, and God has permitted it. For God is not a destroyer, but a preserver of that which His power builds up, and He accounts it as *His ordinance*; for nothing is brought forth which has not existed in eternity.

11. But you must understand it correctly: Hell and Anger are the *Abyss*, which also mingles its wonders therein. We see this where <margin>Or, contrast [foyle].</margin> there is good ground, and the Sower also sows good seed, yet thistles and thorns grow up among it—just as *Christ* gave us a parable of the Sower. As it is in the mind of humanity, so it is also in the Spirit of this world.

Through this, everything became **essential** and **substantial**. The **Tincture** <note>The "Tincture" is a term used by Boehme to describe the spiritual power or "soul-light" that gives quality, color, and life to matter.</note> became substantial and prevailed, and this **substantiality** became material; yet it was not divided, for that cannot be—it is only one single <margin>p substance.</margin>

For through the <margin>r **Word of the Lord**</margin>, the **Tincture** took on **substantiality**, and the **Spirit** of the **substantiality** also took to itself <margin>s a body</margin>; and so there were two sexes or kinds. The body of the **Tincture** had within it the **Centre** of **Life**, but the body of the **substantiality** did not have the **Centre** required for the **igniting of the fire**. It did indeed have life, but <margin>t a weak life.</margin>

<margin>
u Red hot.
</margin>
47. We demonstrate this so that you may rightly understand it: look upon <margin>u glowing iron</margin>, which sends two spirits forth from itself. One is a hot spirit, which possesses the "Centre" and can kindle and awaken another fire. The other is an airy spirit, from which water proceeds; this also has all the **powers** of the **fire**, yet the **Tincture** within it is not fire itself. Instead, it is an **eternal barrier** <note>original: "Barre"</note> that prevents fire from existing within it. And yet, it is the **Spirit** of the **Fire**, having its source out of the **fire** and its life like the **fire**, for in **Eternity** there is no death. Therefore, in the female kind, no life can proceed solely out of their **Tinctures**, but the **Matrix** <note>The womb or generative source.</note> must receive the **Tincture** from the seed of the male.

48. Thus we also declare to you the **basis** <note>original: "Ground"</note> of the difference between the male and the female kind. When **God** created the **material substance**, the **kinds** of all **essences** in the **Centre of Nature** came forth according to all their properties. For as you see the **stars**—that one has a property different from the other—they are all created out of the **Centre of Nature** according to **material substantiality**. So all **essences** stood in the **material substantiality**, and the **Fiat** attracted everything toward the **created earth**.

49. And there, every form of the **Tincture** and of the **Spirit** of the **various kinds** shaped its own body according to its properties—resulting in **beasts**, **birds**, **worms**, **fishes**, **trees**, and **herbs**, as well as **metals** and **earth**, all according to how the life was within them. You may clearly perceive this by the difference of each **day's work** <insert>of the Creation</insert>.

50. For on the first day, **God** created the **material water** (which has a **weak life** and acts as a barrier against the fire of **Anger**),

a Number of a man.

79. The [Spirits of Nature] in the book of *Revelation* are the seven Spirits of Nature: one of them is the Kingdom, and the six are the Center of Nature—that is, the heavenly Center. If this is set down according to the ᵃ *human number*, it makes the number *twelve*; and according to the two kingdoms—namely, the Kingdom of God and the kingdom of this world, out of ᵇ which the faithful are generated—there are then twenty-four spirits. These are the *Elders before the Throne of God* who *worship God and the slain Lamb*. Consider this well.

80. We further explain concerning this world's Center of Nature in this way: the birth of life winds itself like a wheel inwards into itself, and when it comes to the innermost point, it then attains Liberty—yet not the [Liberty] of God, but only the Tincture <note>A spiritual essence or "dye" that gives quality and life to matter.</note> from which life burns.

<margin>c Seize <note>original: "catch hold of"</note>.</margin>
81. For whatever will ᶜ grasp hold of God must pass through the fire; for no substance reaches God unless it subsists in the fire—understand, in a specific fire. If that fire were to kindle, the world would melt away. We do not mean the fire of the "out-birth" <note>The physical, external world produced from the spiritual center.</note>, which is not a true fire but only a sharp intensity that consumes the outward substance springing from the water—namely, *wood and flesh*—but does not affect the inward fire within stone.

<margin>d Outside.</margin>
82. Therefore, observe this: the Liberty ᵈ outside the nature of this world is only eternity *without substance*. Now, just as the eternal center generates itself out of the desiring of the eternal will (as mentioned before), so has the other center of the Third Principle also generated itself [in like manner] through the Word *Fiat* <note>Latin: "Let it be done"—the creative command of God.</note> in the out-birth from the Eternal.

That life on the wheel is ^i \* **Mercury**; for the wheel is always winding around, and Mercury is a stirrer, a maker of sound and noise. Yet he does not have the life himself, for ^\* that exists in the Fire. Therefore, he desires the fierce, stormy turbulency which strikes up the Fire; and that quality on the wheel is ^l **Mars**, who is a rager, a stormer, and the one who ignites the Fire.

This substantiality desires meekness, and that quality on the wheel is ^m **Venus**. For she is the meekness of the outward nature and creates Love; she is what the other five forms desire. Every form winds itself inwards and desires the Liberty of God, which is a meek stillness and like *nothing*, and yet is *All*. And if they have this meekness, which also creates water, then the water is thick and is like a darkness that desires Light and becomes pregnant with it. Thus, the meekness—namely, Venus—has a luster of its own above all the stars in the firmament, for the desiring catches the Light.

88. Now the Light is without substance; it is only still and meek. It desires life and spirit, yet it can produce no life or spirit out of the water and meekness alone. Therefore, Venus desires—with its meekness and light—the Heart; that is, the power and virtue of all the forms. So she catches the Heart, which is the ^n **Sun** located at the point on the wheel. The Sun is the heart of all the six forms, and they are the forms of their Heart, which together constitute a life. Now, if that life were unsteadfast and should ^o pass away as often as the wheel winds around, even if it lasted a long time, it would endure only for a *Seculum* <note>original: "Seculum" (Latin): An age or long period of time.</note>, an age, or a term of *twenty-nine years* <note>Böhme likely refers to the orbital period of Saturn (roughly 29.5 years), which he connects to the cycle of time in nature.</note>.

89. Now the seven forms desire Fire (being the Eighth Number), [and the Eighth Number desires a life] which may continue; for the Heart is not alive without Fire. Therefore, the Heart catches hold of the **Fire**. That fire is fierce and consuming, and it consumes all the seven forms of the **Center** along with their substantiality.

## The Tenth Chapter.

### *Further of the Creation of Every Being: And how Man may seek and find himself: Also how he may find all Mysteries, even to the Ninth Number and no Higher.*

1. **Y**our searching among the stars and elements, supposing you will find the mysteries of nature, is but labor in vain. You find no more than a single perspective <note>original: "one Eye"</note> and see with but one eye; and when you suppose you have found the Sun <note>original: "Sol"</note>, you have scarcely found the Moon <note>original: "Luna"</note>, but only a glimpse of the Sun. You are far from the Heart and do nothing but run with the Moon around the center.

### How Man may find himself. Chapter 10.

<margin>k Sabaoth <note>original: "Zeboath," a Hebrew title for God meaning "of Hosts."</note></margin>
28. ...and have [anything to do] with the *Tenth Number*—that is, to go back into the *Center* of the *Fire of God*. Instead, they greatly rejoice before the *Tenth Number* eternally, and with their songs of *praise* and *Hallelujahs*, they sing: "Holy! Holy! Holy! is our God, the Lord of *Hosts*!" That song is food for the *Divine Fire*, from which goes forth into the *Divine Essences* the *Wonder*, *Paradise*, the *Element*, and *Heavenly Substantiality* <note>The spiritual "matter" or essence of heaven.</note>. It stands before the *Number Three* <note>The Trinity.</note> as a *Virgin* of the *Eternal Knowledge of God*, and that is the *Wisdom of God*. For in *Wisdom* appears the substance of all *Divine Essences*, of which we have no more pen nor tongue to write here.

<margin>l 100th Number.</margin>
<margin>m 1000th Number.</margin>
29. We only show you how far you should search into such a *revelation*. For in the *Ninth Number*, you see all things; it is the *Tincture* <note>original: "Tincture," meaning the spiritual essence or life-force.</note> of the *Heavenly Life*. In the *Ninth Number*, you see the *Hundredth Number* of the *Virgin of Wisdom*, and also the *Thousandth Number* of the *Crown of Majesty*.

30. But you must not search further into the *Tenth Number*, trying to search and dive into the *Abyss*. Otherwise, you go out from God as *Lucifer* did, who wanted to be a *Creator* in the *Tenth Number*. He sought to possess the fire of the *Eternal Origin*, and there he must remain as if in *death* and *darkness* eternally.

<margin>n Or, Matter.</margin>
<margin>o Note.</margin>
31. Therefore, let the reader be warned not to dive further into these very deep writings, nor plunge his will deeper than what he understands <note>original: "apprehendeth."</note>. He should always remain satisfied with his own understanding. For within that understanding, he still stands in <margin>Or, matter.</margin> *substantiality*, and there he <margin>Note.</margin> does not err, no matter how deep the *Spirit* leads him. To one person, more will be given than to another. The only goal <note>original: "Mark."</note> to be aimed at is that everyone remains steadfast in *humility* toward God and submits himself to God, so that He may produce both the *will* and the *deed* as He pleases. When you do that, you are dead to yourself; for you desire nothing but *God's* will, and the will of God is your *life*. This life goes inward even to the *Thousandth Number* and searches into the *Deep of the Deity* with all its *wonders*. He leads your will, when it is submitted to Him, into the *Virgin of His Wisdom* so that you may behold all *wonders*. But you must not leave Him and focus your imagination <note>original: "imagine into," meaning to fix one's desire or creative thought upon something.</note> on the *wonders*. As soon as you do so, you go completely out of *God's* will (which is *Eternal Liberty*) and are held captive by your own *imagination*. Mark this: *every imagination* creates *substantiality*, and that is where you stand. You must go out from that again, or else you shall not see God.

32. Therefore *Christ* teaches us *humility*, *love*, *purity of heart*, and to be *merciful*. He calls upon us to seek the will of God and to submit ourselves to it. For in *God's* will, we are able to do *ALL* things; our own nature must not do it, but God Himself...

# Of the true Knowledge, what Man is. Chapter 11.

6. ...joy [or to perfection] unless it is satisfied with the upper [part]; and then it is *ripe*, for it has attained the body of Venus <note>original: "Venus'es Body"; in the author's alchemical framework, Venus represents a stage of beauty, desire, and the formative power of love</note>. But as the body of Venus is unstable <note>original: "unfixt"</note> and would soon fade if the Sun did not support it with the power of Saturn <note>original: "vertue of Saturne"; Saturn represents the principles of contraction, weight, and stability</note>, so it is also [with the ripe fruit]: it is not durable but soon turns to rottenness. It cannot be preserved long because *Paradise is out of it.*

7. And thus we give you to understand that all creatures have been created out of the lowermost and uppermost life: the matrix <note>the "Matrix" refers to the womb or the primordial source/substance from which things are born</note> of the Earth provided the body, and the constellations provided the spirit. Their life does not reach up into the Sun, for the Earth has attracted the Sun’s power into its matrix; and so all the creatures that dwell upon the Earth *have attained* the power of the Sun and of the stars. But the bodies of birds <note>original: "Fowles"</note> are from the deep above the Earth; and therefore they also delight most to fly in their own matrix. You also see how all beasts put *their* faces and heads forward and downward, looking toward their matrix and desiring only its food; for every life desires its own mother. You may soon perceive this in birds that are close in quality to the Earth, which eat flesh and are birds of prey; for they proceeded from two *mothers* in the creation: namely <note>original: "viz."</note>, from the upper [Mother] and from the Earth.

<margin>
<sup>c</sup> Separately by itself.
<sup>d</sup> In the Wisdom.
<sup>e</sup> Discovered or beheld.
<sup>f</sup> In the testing <note>original: "Proba"; Latin for "test" or "proof," referring here to a state of spiritual trial</note>.
</margin>

8. The *Earth* is a unique center, and therefore was created <sup>c</sup> <margin>separately by itself</margin> on the first day. It is a manifestation <note>original: "out-birth"</note> of the Eternal Substance, a corrupted matrix (corrupted by the fall of Lucifer). In eternity, the Virgin of God’s Wisdom <sup>d</sup> <margin>in the Wisdom</margin> was known within it, <sup>e</sup> <margin>discovered or beheld</margin> where all the *wonders of God* are seen. In the creation, and afterward even until Adam's fall, Paradise sprang up through the Earth; and so the upper center of nature—namely, the Sun’s heart—drew forth paradisiacal fruit out of the Earth. This indeed was not eaten in an *angelic manner* by any creature except *man*. Though indeed, *he had not eaten of it either*, for the longing of the twofold life caught hold of him suddenly; and there he stood until his sleep, in <sup>f</sup> <margin>in the testing</margin> trial, to see whether his will would continue in God and whether he would eat in an angelic manner. But the beginning shows how he stood <note>referring to how Adam failed the test</note>, which we must ponder <note>original: "chew upon"</note>; and therefore we must eat of the Earth, and at last must become food for the Earth.

9. Thus, if you consider it, you may see the creation of God and how God created the other creatures *before* man; and you see how the life of all creatures stands only in the *matrix* out of which they were created.

10. Now you know what *Moses* says: *that God consulted within himself to create a likeness of his essence, an image of himself, that should rule over all the creatures of this world—over beasts, fish, and birds,*

It introduces the creation of Man as a unique image of God meant to rule over other creatures, despite Adam's eventual failure in his spiritual trial.</summary>

# Of the true Knowledge of what Man is. Chapter 11.

...and over all that lives and moves, and says: God said, *Let us make Man according to our Image: and Created Man to be his Image, yes, in the Image of God He created him.* But out of what? Reason asks; and Moses says: God made Man's Body from a piece of Clay.

<margin>The source or quality of his being.</margin>
11. Beloved Reason, behold! Open both your eyes and do not look with only one eye into the hidden mystery of **Humanity**, as has been the custom for a long time until now. Do you hear what *Moses* says? *God placed Man in the Garden of Eden, which he had made, so that he should cultivate and keep it, and Paradise was within it.* Do you now understand the hidden mystery of Man? He was in Paradise during the Creation and was created in [and for] **Paradise**. For Paradise grew up *through* the Earth; and out of *that Paradisiacal Earth*, which contained the heavenly quality, the Body of Adam was created. For it had to be so, seeing as he was to be a Lord over the Earth and all that was earthly, and was meant to reveal the wonders of the Earth. Otherwise, God would have immediately given him an angelic body. But then the tangible substance with its wonders would not have been manifested; therefore, He gave him a tangible body—but not one as dark, rugged, and beastly as ours are, but a Paradisiacal one.

<margin>The Virgin of Wisdom.</margin>
<margin>Note here: all people and all mankind.</margin>
12. You must understand it this way: the Eternal Virgin of Wisdom <note>In the author's mystical theology, the "Virgin of Wisdom" or Sophia represents the divine mirror or blueprint of all things within God</note> stood in Paradise as a figure in which all the *wonders* of God were known. In its own form, it was an image, but without a physical substance like Man's. Within that Virgin, God created the Matrix <note>original: "Matrix"; the womb or source of physical matter</note> of the Earth, so that it became a visible, tangible image in substance. In this image, Heaven, Earth, Stars, and Elements existed in substance; everything that lives and moves was contained within this one image. The Matrix of the Earth could not overpower it [namely, the *Image of Man*], much less could the outward elements do so, because it was higher in rank than all of them. It had received never-fading substance along with the Virgin. The Virgin *was not* brought into the image, but the Matrix of the Earth *was* brought into the Virgin-like image. For the Virgin is eternally uncreated and ungenerated; she is the Wisdom of God and a [reflection, counterpart, and] image of the Deity in the <term>Holy Ternary</term> <note>original: "Ternario Sancto"; refers to the Trinity or the three-fold nature of God's manifestation</note>, according to the Number Three and all the eternal wonders of the eternal center of Nature. She is known in the Majesty and the wonders of God, for she is the one who brings to light the hidden things of the depths of the Deity. Thus, beloved man, you see what you are.

13. Now *Moses* says: *And God breathed the breath of life into his nostrils, and then Man became a living soul.* This is the foundation. Oh, you Universities and high schools, dance about this as much as you can! Show yourselves to be Doctors, Masters, or Bachelors [of Arts] here. If you are what you imagine yourselves to be, then why are you blind in this? Why will you be called Doctors, when...

# Of the true Knowledge of what Man is. Chapter 11.

That is not *exclusively* the breath of God; for He breathed the *Air* into his nostrils, but the Breath of God *cannot* be breathed in from the outside. For God himself is the *fullness* of all things and is already present beforehand wherever any outward thing comes [to pass].

<margin>Assuredly.</margin>
<margin>Or, Desiring.</margin>
<margin>Desired.</margin>
<margin>Or, in.</margin>
14. And the image of the Virgin <note>The "Virgin" or "Sophia" represents Divine Wisdom, a central figure in the author’s mystical cosmology</note>, in which His wonders stood, caused it, so that one Imagination embraced and conceived the other. Indeed, God is *without* substance or longing [for His longing is only Majesty and Liberty]. But the Center of Nature upon the Cross of the wonders longed after the Image which was beheld in the Virgin, where the Spirit of God goes forth into Wisdom, so that Wisdom causes Substantiality <note>original: "Substantiality" — physical reality or concrete existence</note>.

Now, the Spirit breathed in the *Air* from the outside, but it breathed itself forth from the inside into the center of the Heart. For it does not dwell outwardly, but in the Center of Nature; it goes forth *from within*, out of the Deity and into the outward world, and opens [or discloses] an Image according to itself—understand, according to the Center of Nature.

### Ch. 11. Of the true Knowledge of what Man is.

...Number Three <note>A reference to the Trinity</note>, namely into the Eternal Liberty; and there Man became a living soul, with spirit and soul. For the soul has its origin a degree deeper than the Sun, coming out of the Eternal Fire which burns in the Eternal Willing; this willing exists to generate the Heart of God and to exalt His Majesty within the Wonders <note>In Boehme's thought, "Wonders" are the manifestations of God's power and wisdom</note>.

18. Understand us correctly in this way: God's Holy Spirit has *awakened the living soul* out of the Center of Eternal Nature, upon the *Cross* <note>The "Cross" here is a metaphysical symbol for the intersection of divine forces</note>, as a distinct center of its own. It was not awakened out of the Number Three, but out of Eternal Nature—specifically out of the fire of the Center of Nature in the Fifth Form. This is the point where the *two* kingdoms, namely God's Love and Anger, *part*. There, the Spirit of God awakened the soul and brought it forth from within, outwards into the *Tincture* <note>original: "Tincture" — a spiritual essence or transformative quality</note> of the outward spirit, into the blood of the heart, *through Himself; and this is the soul*. Master Doctor <note>Presumably addressing a learned critic or academic contemporary</note>, understand this correctly and do not boast so proudly, grandly, and insultingly; for the soul is the child of God. Its will should always be inclined toward the will of God in the Tenth Number <note>A symbolic number for divine perfection and the kingdom of Heaven</note>, and then it is an Angel and lives in God. It eats of the Word of God, and of the virtue and life of God. *It should not* turn back into the spirit of this world—into the fire of the *Out-Birth* <note>original: "Out-Birth" — the physical, manifested world existing outside the divine center</note>—but rather into the fire of the Number Three, into the *Holy Ternary* <note>original: "Ternarium Sanctum"</note>.

19. And thus you may understand *what you are* and what you were before the Fall. For you could rule over the Sun and the stars; everything was in your power. The Fire, the Air, and the Water, together with the Earth, could not compel you. No outward fire could burn you, no water could drown you, and no air could suffocate or stifle you. Every living thing was in awe of you. You had your own food from the Paradisaical fruit to sustain the outward life, and the *Word of the Lord* <note>original: "Verbum Domini"</note> to sustain the inward life of the soul. You would have lived eternally without sorrow or the feeling of any sickness or disease, in pure joy and delight, and moreover, without care or toil. Your mind was like the mind of a child playing with his father's wonders [or craftsmanship]. *No knowledge* of any evil will was in you; no greed, no pride, no envy, no anger, but all was a play of Love.

<margin>
<image-desc>A circle containing a full cross (horizontal and vertical bars intersecting at the center).</image-desc>
*Adam had the whole Cross in the brainpan of his skull. But now, the Man and the Woman each have only one half of the Cross, like this:*
<image-desc>Two circles side-by-side: the first contains only the vertical bar of a cross, and the second contains only the horizontal bar.</image-desc>
</margin>

20. Now behold, so that you may finally grasp this: God took a particular "day's work" upon Himself regarding Man. If He had intended for Man to be earthly, beast-like, and mortal, He would have created him on the *Fifth Day* with the other beasts. And so that you may clearly understand this: He created only *one Man*, with the *whole Cross in the brainpan, which signifies the Number Three. He was *both Man and Woman*—though you are not to understand this as a physical woman, but as a virgin who was wholly pure and chaste. He contained within him the Spirit of the Tincture of the Fire and also the Spirit of the Tincture...

# Of the True Knowledge of What Man Is. Ch. 11.

...of the Water, namely of Venus: he loved himself, and through himself he loved God. He could generate in a virgin-like manner (out of his own will and out of his essences, without pain and without tearing or dividing his body) a human being just like himself; for he had *all three centers* within him. And just as the center of Eternal Nature was not torn or divided when the Spirit of God conceived his soul upon the Cross and brought it into Wisdom; and just as the spirit of this world was not torn or divided when the Spirit of God breathed into him the spirit of this world as an outward life; so he also was not torn or divided. For he had a body that could pass through trees and stones; if he had remained steadfast in the will of God, then he would have brought **God** <note>original: "H I M"</note> with him into the Great Wonders.

<margin>
Mawerstein <note>original: "Mawerstein" — likely meaning a common building stone or wall-stone</note>
</margin>
21. The noble **Philosopher’s Stone** <note>A legendary substance capable of turning base metals into gold and achieving immortality; here used as a metaphor for spiritual perfection</note> was as easy for him to find as any other stone <margin>p</margin>. He could have adorned his outward life with gold, silver, precious stones, jewels, and pearls—all for his own joy and to manifest God's wondrous deeds. He had no need for animal-like <note>original: "Beastiall"</note> clothing, for he went naked yet was clothed with the **heavenly tincture** <note>In Boehme's alchemy-influenced theology, a 'tincture' is a spiritual essence or life-force</note>. He had no body parts that he would be ashamed of, as his Fall demonstrates. This was his **Fall**: his soul focused its imagination on the outward fire of the "out-birth" <note>The physical, manifested world</note> and the spirit of this world. He turned himself away from God, desiring to live in his own self-centeredness <note>original: "property"</note> and to be his own lord. His will turned away from God’s will and was disobedient; he desired earthly fruit from earthly essences. This caused God to allow the Tree of Temptation to grow, so that He might test what His image [Man] would do, and He forbade him that tree.

<margin>
<image-desc>A circular symbol containing a cross, likely representing Earth or a philosophical principle.</image-desc>
q Wit, wisdom, and understanding.
r The noble Sophia.
</margin>
22. But his desire <note>original: "Lust"</note> immediately pursued the earthly essences of Good and Evil—a process the Devil eagerly encouraged (as he still does) until Adam was overcome. Adam abandoned the will of God and allowed himself to be entirely captivated by the spirit of this world. At that point, everything was over for him: the heavenly image became earthly, and the soul became imprisoned in the fire of God's anger. It no longer possessed the heavenly tincture and could no longer eat of the **Word of the Lord** <note>original: "Verbum Domini"</note>. It had turned toward the spirit of this world and left God's will for the desires of this world. Likewise, at the moment of the Fall, the spirit of this world made the body completely earthly. Paradise, along with all heavenly wit, wisdom, and understanding <margin>q</margin>, withdrew into itself (and so departed from Adam). The dear **Virgin of Wisdom** <margin>r <note>The "Noble Sophia," a personification of divine wisdom</note></margin> remained standing in grief until the **Word** of the Lord came again and looked upon her once more with the promise of the "Seed of the Woman" <note>A reference to Genesis 3:15, traditionally interpreted as a prophecy of the coming of Christ</note>. Then she <margin>s</margin> passed into the light of life and now warns people of their ungodly ways.

...remain in the fire? For no soul can reach God unless it is turned into the will of God and is regenerated <term>regenerated: spiritually reborn</term> in God; otherwise, there is no surviving in the fire.

49. For the soul must be tested through the fire and must not be turned anywhere except into great humility—into the love and <margin>mercy <note>original: Barmhertz-igkeit (German for mercy)</note></margin> **mercy** of God. In the humility of Jesus Christ, it must bring Christ’s body and stand in the wisdom of God, which must be its body; otherwise, it will not be recognized as God’s child. For it must be as pure as it was when it was created upon the **Cross**. It must be regenerated upon the **Cross** of Christ and enter with Christ into the flesh and blood of Christ—through the death of Christ and through the **Anger** of God—into the **Ninth Number** <note>In the author's mystical system, the Ninth Number represents the point of transition from the material or dark world into the light of God's majesty.</note>, as into the **Tincture** <term>tincture: a spiritual essence or transformative power that changes the nature of a thing</term> of the eternal divine fire. There it stands as a creature before the **Tenth Number** <note>The Tenth Number represents the ultimate unity and perfection of God's presence.</note>, as before the Holy Trinity, and humbles itself before the Trinity. Then the majesty of the Trinity embraces it as a dear child. Thus, humility is the food and strength of the majesty, from which the **Brightness** radiates from eternity to eternity.

51. Where will you hypocrites remain, with your **glittering luster** which is generated out of greed and pride? Oh, you children of God, **go out from this Whore** <note>A reference to the "Whore of Babylon," often used by 17th-century writers to symbolize the corrupt institutional church or false religious systems.</note>! She stands on the Devil’s stage and is carried in a show of triumph, to God’s disgrace and contempt.

### The Great Open Gate of the Antichrist.

52. Listen and see, you poor soul; we will show you the very **Antichrist** who dominates the whole world. God has made him known to us so that you might see him; for until now you have considered him a **God**. But now his shame must come to light; for he is so secret that no one knows him unless they are born of God and understand God’s essence and will. Otherwise, he remains hidden in every person—for there is no one who does not have him and carry him **in his heart**. Even if someone is a child of God, yet does not have the deep knowledge of God, he <margin>still depends on him</margin> **still clings** to him. For the Devil has sneaked into him in the form of an angel. Therefore, pay attention to what follows here, for it is **the Number of the Seventh Seal** <note>A biblical reference to the Book of Revelation, suggesting the final unveiling of divine secrets.</note> and <margin>makes known <note>original: Or, maketh knowne.</note></margin> **reveals** the Eternal Day.

53. Observe this, you children of God: for I myself, before the time of my high knowledge, honored and respected him this way and supposed it was according to God’s will. I was taught no differently, and the whole world holds the same view. Though this does not hurt the ignorant—so that they may be saved in their simplicity well enough—yet God will **reveal him** in this last...

46. O you **Antichristian world!** What mischief have you done with your **ceremonies**, in that you have set them in the place of God? If you had declared to sinners God's anger and punishment, and the Devil's false lust, and how a sinner must go forth from his sins into the will of God, and—with true sorrow and **repentance**, in a right trust and confidence—be born again in God; and that God only seeks and desires the **abyss** of the **heart**, namely, the soul; and that all false and wicked lusts and desires must be removed out of the heart—* oh, how well you would have taught!

47. You have established ceremonies and shine in imitation of **Aaron** <note>original: Aaron (the first High Priest of the Israelites, known for his ornate priestly vestments)</note>, but why do you not live in the obedience of Aaron toward God? Everyone looks upon the fine, hypocritical performance of the work, and his heart is carried away toward the hypocrisy; he supposes, when he keeps or celebrates the **ceremonies**, that it is an atonement for the anger of God. But it is **idolatry**, and that which entangles the heart and leads it captive in hypocrisy. It would be better to use **no ceremonies** at all, but simply to perform the express command of God which he has left us in his **Covenant** and **Testament**. The congregation of Christ can well proclaim and sing of God's works of wonder, but best of all in the ¹ **mother tongue**, which everyone understands and can lift up his heart and soul in. In this way, the whole church or congregation of Christ, as one body, exults in God and sings of the wonders of God, which stirs up attention and reflection. In a strange or foreign language <note>This likely refers to the use of Latin in the liturgy, which the author argues excludes the common people from true understanding.</note>, it is but **hypocrisy** and ostentation, by which pride will boast; for pride always willingly appears in a seemingly **divine** posture of hypocrisy. For the Devil is such an idol, for he mocks God his Creator with it, and so portrays the Antichrist before the face of God, so that God should see what a powerful lord and prince he is—one who can shine just as the majesty of God shines. And so he creates such a glittering show in mockery of God and brings the souls of men into that glittering [**hypocritical show of holiness**].

48. O you proud and greedy **Antichrist**, what have you done, that you have thus led yourself and many thousand souls away from God to your own glittering pomp? How will you be able to endure when the **bright face of God appears?** Where will your poor soul turn in your glittering hypocrisy when the **Day of Judgment** shall come? Seeing all must pass through the **fire**, where will your own appearance of holiness remain? Will it not

7. Oh, how well it is for the *Rich* and *Powerful* when the humble and simple *Congregation of Christ* loves and blesses them! Oh, how well it is for a *Teacher and Preacher* who is a true *Minister of Christ*, who gives the food and drink of *Christ* to the *Lambs* committed to his trust and refreshes them with it, so that they yield their souls in obedience to him, and heartily love him, and desire all welfare for him. 9. *Dear Brothers and Sisters* in the *Congregation of Christ*, bear with us. Let us rejoice a little with one another. We bear a hearty *Love* toward you and speak from the *Spirit of our Mother*—from the *Spirit of the Eternal Wisdom of God* [that is, from the Spirit of humility].

<margin>h That is, on the way from Jerusalem, falling among thieves.</margin>

For we are in a *strange country*; we will persuade one another and agree, and we will go home to our own *Country* to our *Mother*. Oh, how she will rejoice when she sees her children come to her in *Eternity*! We will tell her of the *great afflictions* we underwent in *Jericho* <note>In the author's mystical theology, Jericho often symbolizes the fallen, material world or the state of sin from which the soul must journey toward the "spiritual Jerusalem."</note>; we will speak of the great danger we were in among many evil *Beasts*; we will speak of the *Driver* or *Oppressor* who held us captive for so long, and we will speak of how we were freed from him. Let us be of one mind, so that our *Mother* is not grieved or offended by us.

11. *Rejoice, you heavens, with us, and let the earth be glad, for the praise of the **LORD** goes over all mountains and hills! He opens the doors for us so that we may go to our Mother. Let us rejoice and be glad, for we were born blind and now have come to see! Open the Gates*

<page-num>196</page-num>
# Chapter 12: Of the True Christian Life.

...of the LORD, you servants of God, so that the Virgins with their *music* may go in; for that is the dance in which we shall rejoice and be glad with the Virgins, says the Spirit of the LORD of LORDS.

12. O beloved children of men—even *all* who have come forth and been born from *Adam*, in every island and country, wherever you dwell, and by whatever name you are called: *Notice*; the God of Heaven and Earth, who has created us all and brought us forth out of one body, who gives us life and breath, and who preserves our body and soul; He calls us *all* into one love. You have gone astray for a long time, for you have followed human inventions and opinions. The Devil has deceived you so that you hate, persecute, and murder one another, and are utter enemies against one another. Open your eyes and see: do we not all have one and the same breath, and are we not born from *one and the same soul*? We all have one God, whom we honor and worship; that very same God has created us *all*. Moreover, we have one and the same Heaven, which is God’s, and God dwells there. We shall all meet together at the Last Day, all who have trusted in God. Why, therefore, do we dispute for so long about God and His will?

<margin>kernel or seed.</margin>
<margin>of one body.</margin>
13. If we lift up our hearts to Him and yield ourselves to Him in obedience, *then we are all within His will*; no one can thrust us out of it. We all stand in this life as if in a field, and we are growing; the stars and the elements are the field in which we grow. God has sown us in it. *Adam* is the first <gloss>kernel or seed <note>original: "i"</note></gloss> grain that God Himself sowed; and out of that grain we all grow. We are all from one seed; we are all <gloss>of one body <note>original: "*"</note></gloss> own brothers and sisters.

14. But the Devil has sown weeds among us. He has sown no man (for that he cannot do in eternity), but he has blinded us and has sown *pride, envy, anger, greed, and ill-will* [or *malice*] into our minds, to destroy us with them. For he envied <note>original: "grudged"</note> us the privilege of being children of God in the place where he once was. He has fallen away from God through anger, pride, and envy, and has turned himself away from God; therefore, he tries to deceive us so that his own kingdom may be great.

15. O dear children, *do not trust him*; for where God sows His good seed, the Devil follows and sows weeds among it. You see this in the teachings of *Moses* and the *Prophets*, and also in *Christ’s* doctrine. They all preached the way of God in one and the same love and directed us to the living God. They taught that we should leave behind our evil, fleshly lusts—from lying and falsehood, from uncleanness, from greed, and from murder and theft—and enter into a pure, chaste, humble life in the fear of God. We should put our trust wholly in Him as His children and acknowledge Him as our Father, and then He will give us rain and blessing for our body and soul.

<margin>Or, self-interest.</margin>
15. But observe what the Antichristian Devil has sown into it: he has sown pride and self-honor, with state and pomp into it. He has set himself in the chair of *Moses* and of the *prophets*, as also upon the authority of *Christ*, and has led us astray, so that we have made a split and division among us. He has established a *predestination* <note>The doctrine that God has pre-decided who will be saved and who will be damned, which the author here argues causes division.</note> and—regarding the Spirit of God, which has often shown itself in man through wonders and mighty works—he has attempted to create an envious malice, as if God loved one people and hated another, or as if He chose one generation and not another. By this, he (who is called the Devil’s-Christ and *Satan*) sits only in honor and luxury. He has raised wars among the people so that people are at variance, and has set up differing opinions and stirred up the anger of God; for they have gone away from God with their opinions. And so the anger of God has ruled over them and oftentimes destroyed them; for that which has no good in it, God will never endure in His country, but gives it up to anger—though indeed it runs headlong into it and whets the sword, so that one people devours another. *From the beginning of the world to this time*, all contentions, disputes, and wars, as also envy and malice, have arisen from *Antichrist*, who wants to be honored as a God in the form of an angel—and the Devil dwells in him.

<margin>Whom.</margin>
17. This *Antichrist* is plainly to be discerned by *Cain* and *Abel*: in that *Cain* slew his brother for the sake of faith [and religion]. For *Abel* had set his heart upon God and had committed himself to God, whom God loved and whose sacrifice He accepted. But *Cain* had set his heart upon this world and wanted to be a lord on Earth; his mouth gave God good words, but his heart stuck fast in an earthly conceit [and opinion]. He loved the spirit of the *Mammon* <term>Mammon: material wealth or greed, often personified as a false god.</term> of this world, and the Devil slipped into it. And so his sacrifice was not acceptable to God, but the smoke fell down to the Earth, and the Devil accepted his sacrifice. And so he slew his brother by the Devil’s instigation and in his own false conceit and opinion: he desired the glory, honor, and power of this world, and *Abel* desired the love and grace of God.

18. Thus, dear people all over the Earth, you see that you are all of one flesh; but you have divided yourselves one from another, which the Devil in the *Antichrist* has brought to pass. Your fear of God has many times been great, and you have done

That order which had many rich properties and revenues had to give much to the chief *devil* so that he might be fat and a lord upon earth. The simple laypeople were persuaded these *orders* were holiness itself, and so they worshipped before the dragon in the Book of Revelation and sought pardon, absolution, and the forgiveness of sins from them. Neither shall it be held against you or accounted to your debt at the Last Day (for you proceeded blindly in it, even though on that day the Holy Martyrs shall be set before your eyes). 22. Yet behold and observe what a zealous will, or an earnest desire, can do if one enters into the will of God with their whole heart. Even if a person does not know what they are doing and is <term>zealous in a strange opinion</term><note>The author refers to being passionate about an unorthodox or incorrect religious doctrine.</note>, if their heart is directed toward God and they believe steadfastly in their ignorance that it is pleasing to God, miracles can happen. 23. The Antichrist has insinuated himself into these wonders and has created almost as many <margin>e Tenets or Sects and Orders of Religion.</margin> opinions as there are days in the year. Among the believers who have believed so blindly in their particular opinion, even wonders and miracles have been performed. The Antichrist has attributed these to the "opinion" itself, whereas the opinion could not even make a fly move. Rather, it was the firm and strong faith—which moved beyond the opinion toward God—that <margin>u Or, performed the miracles.</margin> awakened those wonders. For the Spirit of God is found in faith, not in opinion, and faith comes from God. When the soul inclines itself toward God while holding an opinion, it lays hold of the Spirit of God. The <term>opinion</term><note>The specific religious dogmas or sects that often divide people.</note> is like the fire; but the soul does not stay in the fire. Instead, it presses out from there into God, blossoming out of the fire like a beautiful flower growing out of the earth.

24. These opinions were tolerable enough to God, and God did not reject them as long as the soul sought God through the opinion, and as long as the Church of Christ <margin>x Or, had a true Government.</margin> maintained a proper governance. But when the Devil crept into it and turned it into a grand, glittering kingdom—when the priests sought only honor, greed, and luxury, and led people away from God toward their own <margin>y The performance of ceremonies.</margin> works—the opinions became completely blind. Because they themselves departed from God into the works of their own hands, following forged and invented paths, God let them go, seeing they would not be directed by His Spirit.

25. And Asia, Africa, and Greece are to be considered fortunate because they have returned from the "works of men" to the one and only God again. # Chapter 13: Of Christ’s Testaments.

40. [Pro]pagated yourselves, and not the office of Christ; but you have now become very few in Germany. Where there were a thousand of you, there are now scarcely a hundred. If you will not leave behind your human wit and your own inventions, God will so reject you that where there are now a hundred, there shall not remain ten of you, nor even a smaller number. Awake from your sleep, lest you go down into perdition into the abyss.

41. You say we laugh you to scorn: it may be you think so, for there is one who laughs you to scorn whom we know, and who shows it to us. He will suddenly awake; do not be so complacent and careless, but consider it. For no one takes anything for himself unless it is given to him by God, nor will this be spoken in vain.

<margin>Note.</margin>
42. O beloved and worthy Christendom, observe it well: do not say, "if our teachers do not lead us correctly, let them deal with it." O no, it concerns your very self; it will cost the loss of your body and soul. Dear Christendom has departed from all the apostolic <term>Apostolic: Relating to the original twelve apostles and their simple, spiritual teachings.</term> ordinances, virtue, and power, into human inventions and institutions. In stead of Christ's Kingdom, a pompous, stately, and hypocritical one has been set up through Baptism and the Lord's Supper.

<margin>Godly or pious.</margin>
43. Men set up ceremonies: O, if they had kept the true faith and had shown the people the divine way toward the new regeneration <note>original: "New Regeneration"; in this context, it refers to the spiritual rebirth of the soul through Christ.</note>, if they had shown them the clear countenance of God, then people would have departed from their sins into a divine life.

<margin>Or, husband.</margin>
44. If my eyes had not been opened by God, how should I have known you? I would indeed have still worshipped you. The world shall seek you and at length find you; and then Europe shall be a crown, and Asia the husband, and Africa the country, and a simple shepherd shall lead us to pasture. If you understood this, you would look into yourself; but you will be blind until you are repaid. As you have poured out affliction, so you shall drink up misery and torment, for you have made it so very great as it is. You are a wild tree and shall be broken off. There is no remedy; your own wrath casts you to the ground, for you are weighed in a balance and are found too light, says the Spirit of the Great Wonders.

<margin>p The Eternal Nature.</margin>
53. Therefore says the p <term>Mother of the Genetrix</term> <note>original: "Genetrix," a Latin term for the creative or "begetting" power of nature</note>, "I am in anguish; I had planted me a little tree and desired to eat of its good fruit, but it has borne much wild fruit, which I have no mind to eat. I will generate him out of the first root, and will break the <term>Turba</term> <note>original: "Turba," a Latin term used by the author to describe the spirit of confusion, disorder, or divine wrath that destroys</note>, for q its number in the crown is accomplished."

<margin>q The highest pitch of the apostate children.</margin>
54. What do you seek so much, you wild branches? You say you are above the Mother, [above the Spirit of God]; you have art, knowledge, and learning. What delight has the Mother in your wit and art? She desires no art and wit, for she is altogether simple and counts only [the number] one. If you would please the Mother, you must go from multiplicity into *one* again—not through art and wit, but you must go forth out of your proud *Turba*, out of self, into simple humility. You must leave the <note>original: "bravery," meaning outward show or ostentation</note> bravery and hypocrisy of your own wit that proceeds from the *Turba*, and become as children; otherwise you are not acceptable to your first Mother, but the *Turba* takes you up. And then consider where you shall remain, *when God shall judge the secrets of mankind, when all shall pass through the fire of His wrath, says the Spirit of the Great Wonders.*

55. Mother *Eve* said, when she brought forth the first child: "Behold! *I have the Man, the Lord*" <note>A reference to Genesis 4:1</note>; she thought he would bring about the breaking of the serpent's head and possess the kingdom, but it was *Cain* the murderer.

56. And thus also you now say, "We have found the Lord; now we will possess the kingdom, for we have found the true doctrine; we will teach thus and thus, and then we are God's children." But listen! You have indeed found the true *doctrine*, but you are *Cain*. You look after the kingdom, and not the power and virtue of *Abel’s* sacrifice. You desire only to continue in fleshly pleasure, and retain only the *shell* of God's Word, which has no virtue or power. You retain the *history* (and contend about it, and so destroy your country and people), but you deny the power of it. You say, "We are *near* to the kingdom of God," and are yet far from it, which your *end* will testify.

Know-ledge is the snare that catches you. If you were *simple*, you would not be so proud. What do the simple know concerning false, subtle, and cunning deceit, unless they learn it from the wit of the <term>Turba</term> <note>original: "Turba," a Latin term used by the author to describe the spirit of confusion, disorder, or divine wrath that destroys</note>? Do you say that you have God's will and teach it? Are you not *Cain*, who murders *Abel* every day? Consider yourself well; you are him indeed. *Abel* lies at your feet and begs you, but you are that evil beast that treads *Abel* underfoot. You ride over those on bended knees and consider the poor and simple to be nothing but dirt and dung; yet you devour his sweat and labor and fill yourself with deceit beyond measure. How then do you dare to say, "*Here is the Church of Christ*"? Oh, you are *Babel* <note>original: "Babell," referring to the biblical Tower of Babel, used here to symbolize a state of spiritual confusion and corruption</note>, that city of prostitution and falsehood.

58. You know the will of God, yet you do only your own will. Furthermore, you say, "*We have gone out from Babel; we have the true teaching* [or <gloss>doctrine</gloss>] *among us.*" Indeed, if you had the spirit of righteousness and truth and would be content with a little, then the Mother would always give you enough; you would lack nothing. But your pride and arrogance do not trust God; therefore, you trust only in greed and are eager to devour the fat of the earth. You take it by force and not by right. The "right" you produce, claim, and plead has only been invented by your covetous heart; you live only in deceit. You persuade and deceive yourself to your own loss. If you had wisdom and understanding, you would have respect for your end and what will follow hereafter. But you blindfold *yourself* with pride and say: "Behold, these are *golden times*; many have desired to see what we see and hear what we hear, but have not seen or heard it." Listen! Indeed, that will be a witness against you and will make your judgment heavier. You have not been better for it so far, but worse. Therefore, know that what is declared to you [by the <gloss>Reformation</gloss>] is by your own Prophet, who has called you back from your pride into your mother, *Humility*. But you have become worse and worse. You have broken the sword of the Spirit so that you may do whatever you please. But he has left you and given you up to the <term>Turba</term>, which shall devour you, as was done of old to *Israel*. There is no counsel or *remedy* to help. Your covenants are all worth nothing while you rely upon the *arm of flesh* <note>a biblical idiom referring to human strength or military power as opposed to divine aid</note>, and so God has also departed from you and leaves you to devour yourselves.

59. Or why do you take the *Covenant* of God into your mouth? Since you hate to be *reformed* and thirst after greed, do you suppose God to be a false hypocrite and liar like you? Stop your shouting; you are not acceptable to God unless you turn away and leave your falsehood.

<header>Chapter 14. *The Broad Way and the Narrow Way.*</header>

60. It is with you now according to the driving of the <term>Turba</term> <note>original: "Turba," a term used by the author to describe a spirit of confusion, disorder, or the "wrathful" element of the divine nature that destroys</note>, which takes its recreation in accomplishing the anger of God and devouring what has grown in its kingdom; and you are blind concerning it and *do not see it*. Why are you so covetous? Only go out from it! Do you not see how the <margin>The Blossom of Life.</margin> <gloss>t</gloss> *Noble Tincture* <note>original: "Noble Tincture," an alchemical and mystical term referring to the restorative spiritual essence or the "light" of God within nature</note> has raised itself up? It is near its blossoming, and then you *will* have silver and gold enough.

61. But what shall we say? You have committed spiritual adultery until you have fallen asleep. You go down alive into the <term>Abyss</term> <note>original: "Abysse," referring to the bottomless pit or the state of separation from God</note> rather than forsake the prostitute <note>referring to the corrupt systems of "Babel"</note>; and therefore it shall be done to you just as your own prophet *testifies*, who has called you for a very long time with his trumpet. You wait for [deliverance by] the fiery sword, which will also cut you in pieces.

62. Or do you suppose us to be mad because we speak this way? Indeed, we are born out of you; we see and understand the *complaint* of our Mother, who reproves her children. For she declares the wrath in the *Turba*, which has grown up into the fierce wrath of God.

63. We speak what is given to us, which we know in the *zeal* of the Lord. What have we to do with <margin>Confusion, strife, and jangling.</margin> <gloss>t</gloss> <term>Babel</term> <note>original: "Babell," the biblical city of confusion, used here to represent the corrupt, divided state of the church and world</note>? We speak to ourselves and to the fellow members of our body, and to those who dwell in the courts of God—with those *who at present mourn with us*, whose mourning shall be turned into joy.

# The Fourteenth Chapter.
## *Of the Broad Way in this World which leads us into the Abyss.*
## *And of the Narrow Way [that leads] into the Kingdom of God.*

<margin>I.</margin> Dear children of God, let us heartily and seriously *consider* from where we come, where we are going, and what we do and purpose, so that we may not lose the *eternal and highest Good*. Why do we labor so much after temporary pleasure and luxury, after honor, money, and goods? Are we not strange guests and pilgrims in this world, who should continually expect the end of this life? We are not created for the pleasure and lust of this life, but for <term>paradisiacal joy</term> <note>original: "Paradificall Joy," the original bliss of Eden and the heavenly state</note>, and to lead

<sig>F f 2</sig>
<meta>catchword: a simple life</meta>

<summary>The author warns that humanity is currently blinded by greed and the "Turba" (divine wrath), urging a retreat from the corruptions of "Babel." # The Broad Way and the Narrow Way. Chapter 14.

...a simple, childlike life. We should not know any pomp, state, or haughtiness, but live together *as children* in a loving spirit of joy. We have gone out from our true, pure, *Paradisiacal Mother* <note>original: "Paradificall Mother," referring to the divine source or the state of innocence and spiritual unity with God</note>, in whom we should live as dear *and loving children*.

2. We are shut up in the Mother [the temporary nature] which generates the evil beast, and we have received beastly <note>original: "beastiall"</note> properties. We act no differently than evil beasts; we have given ourselves up to a strange mother who educates us and leads us captive in her bonds. In the end, we must leave the outward man to the earthly mother, for we cannot get away from her because she has captured us in flesh and blood. She breeds us and brings us *up within herself* and keeps us as her children. But yet, we have a very precious *jewel* hidden within us, by which we are God’s children. With that jewel, let us strive for the highest good, so that we may attain it.

<margin>A note.</margin>
3. Dear children, our struggle for the highest good does not consist in the sword—in killing and flaying—so that we should make wars and fight for the cause of God and his kingdom, *and so persecute and murder one another*. Neither does it consist in much *knowledge*, but merely in a simple, childlike obedience: that we should depart from the will of our flesh, which is *beastly*, where the devil dwells, and enter into the will of God. It lies in no man’s opinion or knowledge; <sup>a</sup> for the Spirit of God gives knowledge *out of the wonders* to everyone from whom he is born.

<margin>
b Or, into.
c Or, body.
</margin>
<margin>d Of the spirit of the outward world.</margin>
4. You see how we are put under subjection to the spirit of this world. For when a child is sown <sup>b</sup> in its mother’s <sup>c</sup> womb, *that spirit is there ready and forms the child according to the wheel of the outward nature. That spirit gives condition, will, and disposition to it; it shows the child the wonders of its secret mystery and opens the way of its will* <sup>d</sup> *to the child. It leads the child into its mother, and out of its mother through this world; it gives the child's body to the earth and its soul to hell.*

<margin>e A godly or divine life.</margin>
5. Therefore, since we know this, we ought to lift up the spirit of our soul and *make war only against* that evil earthly spirit. We must oppose it with our soul and body, *and not against our brothers and sisters*. We cannot overcome the devil with disputing and knowing much; neither can we maintain God’s word *with wars and the sword*, but with the simple, obedient <sup>e</sup> *life of God*. Let us be contented with the little we have and depart from the evil lust for pride into an humble, childlike life, where everyone should perform their work with all diligence for *the benefit and profit of their brothers and sisters*, striving thereby to serve God their creator and to please their brother—not seeking...

blood and flesh, which makes the <term>Tincture</term> <note>original: "Tincture"; in this context, a spiritual essence or transformative power that bridges the physical and spiritual worlds</note>, though the Tincture is not blood and flesh, but a virgin **without procreating**; yet the six Spirits in the Tincture continually generate one another, as was mentioned before concerning the Center of Nature. But the brightness of the noble pearl of the soul is especially known in the Tincture; for there it attains God’s power and Spirit. 27. The six spirits of Nature contain the **Eternal Center**, within which the **Darkness** and **Anger** of God are understood; for the **origin** of **movement** <note>original: "Mobility"</note> consists therein. The fire exists there, though indeed it consists of only four forms; in the **fifth** form the true Life springs up, and in the **sixth** comes understanding. Then, finally, in the **seventh**, there is another Spirit, which is not the Center in the **source of anguish** [or property]. For in the seventh form there is another property; indeed, the first six forms rule within it and are the life of the source and a cause of that life, but they **together make one Spirit**, which lives in the blood, water, and air.

28. And though it is true that through the heavy Fall of **Adam**, we are brought into the outward dominion so that the soul swims in the <term>palpable</term> <note>physical or touchable</note> [or visible water], yet the Eternal Water—that is, the Mother of the Water—is hidden in the outward world, in which the soul is **an Angel**. We want you to understand that the soul is a spirit, just as God the Holy Ghost is, who goes forth from the Father and the Son and is the **movement of the Deity**. For the Father stands still and has moved himself only once, namely, in the Creation; but the Spirit has the Word of the Father and performs all things through the Word.

<margin>namely, the soul.</margin>
29. And thus also, the soul is a spirit generated out of the Eternal Center of Nature, out of its own spirits of its own Nature (not strange ones), **which has the word**. This word comprises itself in the six forms of Nature upon the wheel of the Cross and **performs all things** through the Word. For it is the Spirit and Life of the Word, and moves upon the wings of the wind as a flash or blaze; it forms the Word and produces it, and the **Six Spirits** are its counselors. Though there are actually only **five**, for the **sixth** is the form of the Word itself, while the **five** contain the **five senses**.

30. Here we woefully find, and have great cause to lament, how

...how our father **Adam** has here introduced the evil, poisonous, earthly dominion. Because of this, the poor soul is wholly captivated by the spirit of this world, which flows forth and works powerfully in the soul. Thus, frequently and hourly, the evil of the **Abyss** breaks out from the **Word of the soul** <note>The "Word of the soul" refers to the soul's expressive power or its inner creative essence, which should mirror the Divine Word.</note>. The Devil mingles himself into this and possesses our hearts outwardly, and then also most inwardly—namely, in the **first four forms of nature**—and turns us away from the will of God and into all the abominations and wickedness within himself. 

As the Devil now observes how a person is naturally inclined—specifically, which spirit is predominant according to the governance of his body (whether it be pride, covetousness, envy, wrath, unchastity, wantonness, voluptuousness, or such things)—he * **assaults** him continually. He works such great wickedness with the soul as no tongue can express; for in the outward dominion there are also **seven forms**—namely, the **seven planets** which rule the outward man and reach into the bottom of the soul, if the soul does not unceasingly resist and reject the evil, malignant † **influences**. In these influences, the Devil has powerful access to the soul, but he does not yet have full dominion or complete power there unless the <term>Turba Magna</term> <note>original: "Turba Magna"; Latin for "Great Disturbance." In this context, it refers to the awakening of God's anger or a chaotic spiritual upheaval that brings judgment.</note> in the anger of God is kindled—as seen in judgments, pestilence, thunder, and similar plagues and punishments—where he acts as the executioner. However, he does hold the **inward** dominion of the **four forms** belonging to the **fire-life**; he can possess these as often as the soul plunges itself into them. If he catches the soul there, oh, how fast he holds it and tries to drag it down, for that is his kingdom: the abyss of the four forms.

<margin>¶ Sharp astringent.</margin>
31. Observe this according to its precious depth: the **four forms** contain within them the **original of nature**. First, in the desiring will, the darkness enters into a desire along with the "attracting." So the desiring becomes strong, ¶ **harsh**, hard, and cold. This desiring creates an attraction and stirring within that strong harshness—these are the first two forms. The third form is the **great anguish**, arising because the desiring wants to be free; this stirs the anxious **wheel of nature** and, in the end, stirs up the **flash of fire**, which is the fourth form, as was mentioned at length before.

32. And so, that harsh attraction in the desiring of the will creates a great **covetousness** in the outward nature of this world. This causes the mind to want to attract everything to itself and possess it alone. Even if the mind cannot devour it, it still wants to own it and would not willingly give anything to anyone else. This is one root of the **abyss of Hell**, where the Devil vehemently assaults the soul so that it might not escape and come to the **light of God**.

40. For it is sufficiently known to us (seeing God is purely good) that he created nothing evil; for that which was not from eternity, was not in the creation.

<margin>e Quality, or living faculty.</margin>
41. God created no *Hell*, nor any *Devils*, but Angels: only Lucifer has turned himself away from meekness, and has flown out above the Cross of the Number Three <note>original: "Number Three"; a reference to the Trinity</note>, and has himself awakened the fire of anger in the flash <note>original: "flash"; in this context, the sudden ignition or manifestation of divine power or wrath</note>; which had remained *hidden in secret* from eternity; which is now his hell and habitation. He can now be nothing other than covetous, envious, anxious, and wrathful; there is no other $^e$ property or source in him; for his own Mother <note>In the author's mystical theology, the "Mother" refers to the source or nature from which a being is birthed</note>, out of which he was brought forth and created, *holds him now*, so that he is a devil with all his legions.

42. Therefore, dear children, since we know that we are thus surrounded with hell and the devils, in the anger of God, it is very necessary for us to fly *into humility*: and *therefore* Christ teaches us so very earnestly to study meekness, love, and mercy, that we should love one another, and should not so eagerly strive after the spirit of this world; for the devil slips into it and seduces us. We should watchfully take heed of pride, for the devil flies into it; and of *anger*, for that is the devil's sword, with which he commits all murders.

<margin>f Incarnate.</margin>
43. Oh, how lamentable a thing it is that the poor soul is *so* blinded that it does not know the heavy shackles and bonds in which it lies captive. The fire of hell rises up to its very lips; the *whole world* is full of snares which the devil has laid to catch the poor soul. If the eyes of the outward man should be opened, he would be terribly frightened; *Everything* that man touches or looks upon has a net and snare of the devil in it. And if the *Word of the Lord* <note>original: "Verbum Domini"</note>, which has $^f$ become man, were not in the middle—so that the hidden eternal substantiality of the Word is a body—*no one would be saved*; the devil would catch and devour *all* souls.

44. Therefore, dear children, Christ has rightly told us, *That the Kingdom of God in us is as small as a grain of mustard seed*: but he who endeavors seriously and strives after it, to him it grows great, as a *tree*, and the devil must certainly leave it alone; and though he often breaks off a twig, yet the trunk still stands.

This is because the soul enters into lust and into the dominion of this world. For if the soul completely surrenders itself to the lust, pleasure, and dominion of this world, then the Devil does not *sift* <margin>* Or, tempt.</margin> it so intensely. Instead, he carries it in his ᵍ *triumphant chariot* <margin>ᵍ Or, wedding chariot.</margin> from one abomination and wickedness to another. *His chariot is Venus—that is, the love of the flesh*—in which the soul continually strives for temporary power, authority, and honor; for riches, beauty, and the desires of the flesh; and for animal-like, inordinate sexual intercourse. Actually, the soul does not desire these things so eagerly unless it is *totally infected*. It is only because the soul in *Adam* lusted after it and became captivated by it. The Devil continually stirs it up, tickling the soul with these desires so that it might confidently and freely eat the forbidden fruit.

46. We find that human life is *threefold*, with three spirits together in *one*, as if they were but one spirit—and indeed, it is but one life. However, it has three "dominions" <note>original: "Dominions"; spheres of influence or ruling principles</note>, each of which has its own "Mother" <note>The source or origin of a specific nature</note> that provides or generates it. The "center of nature" with its forms (or properties) is the eternal life, for it is the "fire-life." The spirit that is generated and goes out from the center of nature, dwelling in the *tincture* <note>original: "Tincture"; a mystical term for the spiritual essence or 'dye' that gives life its quality</note>, is the eternal <margin>ʰ Life of the soul.</margin> ʰ life of the soul. Finally, the "air-spirit"—with the qualities or properties of the dominion of the stars—is the beginning, ending, and temporary life. This is the "beastly" life (the animal life which we have in common with animals).

47. Now, the soul is generated only from the *first two* spirits, and the *third* is breathed into it. This was not done so that the soul should enter into the third spirit and surrender to it (as Adam did), but so that the soul should mightily rule over it. Through this, the soul was meant to reveal the great wonders of God, which were seen from eternity in the Wisdom of God. For the *third* dominion is generated and created out of the *first*; and the *second* dominion was intended to stay in its own place (in the noble tincture) in Paradise, revealing the great wonders in the third. Therefore, man was made *Lord* over all things. He held the *tincture* (or life) of the earth in his own hand (or power), and *gold and silver* were as easy for him to find as any other visible thing. The tincture of the earth was his ornament and plaything, in a completely childlike way, without greed. He needed no other clothing; and just as gold was pure without dross, his childlike mind was also pure.

48. But the Devil awakened the *sulfur* <note>original: "Sulphur"; in Böhme’s alchemy, this represents the gross, material, or flammable quality of nature</note> (or gross matter) within him and placed the beastly spirit in the dominant position. That which man should have ruled over now rules over him, and that is his *Fall*.

49. There it stands before the Gate of Heaven in a very great Deep in great danger; its root is the Anger of God and Hell-fire, and its superior or predominant spirit is the dominion of this world. There it stands in the *Tincture* <note>original: "Tincture"; a mystical term for the spiritual essence or 'dye' that gives life its quality</note> of the Fire, in the *midst*; and whichever way it inclines, *there* it enters. If it goes into the lust and pleasure of this world, then it stands there and is captivated by the Devil; but if it enters into itself inwards into God, then the Devil will *buffet* <note>original: "buffet it"; to strike, harass, or struggle against</note> it, for then it is in his country.

<margin>Shame and disgrace.</margin>
50. But when the soul obtains the flesh of Christ as a new Body, then it is *not* in the Devil's country; that is a Tree before him which is poison and death to him, by which he is vexed and reluctant to touch. But he stirs up his servants and ministers *against the outward body*, which must bear <margin>Shame and disgrace.</margin> reproach and scorn, so that he may cover and hide this Tree so it may not be known. Otherwise, it might bring forth more branches, and eventually, Hell would be too narrow for him; therefore, he will prevent it as long as he can.

<margin>Snares or traps.</margin>
51.
